華潤集團於中國的城市燃氣分銷旗艦                THE PRC CITY GAS DISTRIBUTION FLAGSHIP
華潤燃氣控股有限公司（「華潤燃氣」）主要由中國最大
                                OF CHINA RESOURCES GROUP
的國有企業集團之一的華潤（集團）有限公司擁有。作為       China Resources Gas Group Limited (“CR Gas”), substantially
中國領先的燃氣公用事業集團，華潤燃氣主要經營業務        owned by China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited, one of
包括天然氣購買及銷售、管道設施建設及運營、綜合服        the largest state-owned conglomerates in China, is a leading gas
務、冷熱電綜合能源、車船用氣等。華潤燃氣的業務策        utilities group in China, the principal businesses of which include
略性地分佈於全國各地，主要位於經濟較發達和人口密        the purchases and sales of natural gas, the construction and
集的地區以及天然氣儲量豐富的地區。               operation of pipeline facilities, comprehensive service, combined
                                cooling, heating and power, and gas for vehicles and vessels, etc.
憑藉良好的行業基礎及本集團的執行能力，華潤燃氣於        Its operations are strategically located in areas of China, which are
二零二三年繼續通過內涵式增長及收購實現快速擴張。        economically more developed and densely populated and areas
於二零二三年末，華潤燃氣的城市燃氣項目總共達276       with rich reserves of natural gas.
個，分佈於中國 25 個省份，其中包括 15 個省會城巿及

                                the ecological environment and promoting the transition to green

                                The natural gas market in China is still in the important
                                development period, as China remains the most important
                                region in the global rise of natural gas development. CR Gas
                                is well placed to take advantage of such a favorable operating
                                environment and to become the most respectable leader in the
                                PRC’s gas industry by continuously expanding through organic
                                and external growths; diligently enhancing its operational
                                efficiency in pursue of excellence; delivering a safe and reliable
                                supply of clean energy as well as the caring, competent and
                                efficient service to our customers; offering growing and sustainable
                                performance to shareholders; creating extensive professional
                                and personal development opportunities to employees; and
                                contributing to the protection and improvement of the Chinese and
                                global environment.

守則條文C.3.3 規定，本公司應有正式的董               Under the code provision C.3.3, the Company should have formal
事委任書，訂明有關彼等委任的主要條款                   letters of appointment for directors setting out the key terms and
及條件。本公司並沒有向董事發出正式的                   conditions of their appointment. The Company did not have formal
委任書，惟彼等須根據細則至少每三年輪                   letters of appointment for Directors. However, the Directors are
值退任一次。再者，董事須參考由公司註                   subject to retirement by rotation at least once every three years in
冊處出版之《董事責任指引》及由香港董事                  accordance with the Bye-Laws. In addition, the Directors are required
學會出版之《董事指引》及《獨立非執行董事                 to refer to the guidelines set out in “A Guide on Directors’ Duties”

董事會成員多元化政策                  BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
本公司於二零一三年八月二十三日採納董          The Company adopted a board diversity policy (the “Policy”) on 23rd
事會成員多元化政策（「政策」）並於二零二        August, 2013 and updated the Policy on 26th December, 2022. A
二年十二月二十六日進行了更新修訂。此          summary of this Policy, together with the measurable objectives set for
政策之概要連同為執行此政策而制定之可          implementing this Policy, and the progress made towards achieving
計量目標及達標進度於下文披露。             those objectives are disclosed as below.


政策概要                        Summary of the Policy

本公司視董事會層面的多元化為維持競爭          The Company sees diversity at Board level as an essential element in
優勢的重要元素。一個真正多元化的董事          maintaining a competitive advantage. A truly diverse Board will include
會將包括具備不同技能、地區及行業經           and make good use of differences in the skills, regional and industry
驗、背景、性別及其他特質的董事會成           experience, background, gender and other qualities of the members
員，並可加以利用。該等差異將於釐定董          of the Board. These differences will be considered in determining
事會的最適合組成時予以考慮，並於可能          the optimum composition of the Board and when possible should be
情況下保持適當平衡。董事會所有委任均          balanced appropriately. All Board appointments are made on merit, in
按董事會整體有效運作所需要的技能及經          the content of the skills and experience the Board as a whole requires
驗水平作出。                      to be effective.

     董事會已採納了有效機制，以確保董事會                         The Board has adopted effective mechanisms to ensure independent
     可獲得獨立的觀點及意見。經董事會主席                         views and input are available to the Board. Subject to approval of
     批准後，在適當情況下，董事如有需要可                         the Chairman of the Board, Directors may seek, at the Company’s
     尋求獨立於本公司顧問的獨立法律、財務                         expense, independent legal, financial or other professional advices
     或其他專業意見，費用由本公司承擔，以                         from advisors independent from those advising the Company as and
     促使其有效履行其職責。董事會將每年檢                         when necessary in appropriate circumstances to enable them to
     討該機制的實施及有效性。                               discharge their responsibilities effectively. The Board will review the
                                                implementation and effectiveness of such mechanisms on an annual
                                                basis.

     獲提供獨立觀點及意見。                                ensuring that independent views and input are provided to the Board.

     提名政策                                       Nomination Policy

     提名政策旨在確保董事會在技能、經驗、                         Nomination policy aims to ensure that the Board has a balance
     知識及多元化觀點方面取得平衡，切合本                         of skills, experience, knowledge and diversity of perspectives
     公司的業務要求。                                   appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s business.


     提名委員會將會妥為考慮以下條件（統稱為                        The Nomination Committee will evaluate, select and recommend
     「該等條件」）以評核、甄選及向董事會建議                       candidate(s) for directorships to the Board by giving due consideration
     一名或多名候選人擔任董事，該等條件包                         to criteria including but not limited to (collectively, the “Criteria”):
     括但不限於：


     (a)   多元化觀點，包括但不限於性別、年                     (a)   Diversity in aspects including but not limited to gender, age,
           齡、文化背景及教育背景、專業經                            cultural and educational background, professional experience,
           驗、技能、知識及服務年期；                              skills, knowledge and length of service;


     (b)   就可用時間及有關利益而言，對於董                     (b)   Commitment for responsibilities of the Board in respect of
           事會的職責的承擔；                                  available time and relevant interest;


     (c)   於本公司業務所涉及的行業之成就及                     (c)   Accomplishment and experience in the relevant industries in
           經驗以及其他專業資格；                                which the Company’s business is involved and other professional
                                                      qualifications;


     (d)   誠信方面的聲譽；                             (d)   Reputation for integrity;


     (e)   該（等）人士可以為董事會帶來的潛在                    (e)   Potential contributions that the individual(s) can bring to the
           貢獻；                                        Board;


     (f)   對於董事會繼任有序予以落實的一項                     (f)   Plan(s) in place for the orderly succession of the Board.
           或多項計劃。


     上述因素只供參考，並不旨在涵蓋所有因                         The above Criteria are for reference only, and not meant to be
     素，也不具決定性作用。提名委員會可決                         exhaustive and decisive. The Nomination Committee has the discretion
     定提名任何其認為適當的人士。                             to nominate any person, as it considers appropriate.


     提名委員會將會妥為考慮以下條件以評核                         The Nomination Committee will evaluate and recommend retiring
     及向董事會建議一名或多名退任的董事接                         Director(s) to the Board for re-appointment by giving due
     受再度委任，條件包括但不限於：                            consideration to the criteria including but not limited to:


     (a)   該（等）退任的董事對於本公司的整體                    (a)   The overall contribution and service of the retiring Director(s)
           貢獻及服務，包括但不限於出席董事                           to the Company, including but not limited to the attendance
           會的會議及 ╱ 或其屬下委員會的會議                         of the meetings of the Board and/or its committees and
           及股東大會（如適用），以及在董事會                          general meetings where applicable, in addition to the level
           及 ╱ 或其屬下委員會的參與程度及表                         of participation and performance on the Board and/or its
           現；                                         committees;


     (b)   該（等）退任的董事是否繼續符合該等                    (b)   Whether the retiring Director(s) continue(s) to satisfy the Criteria.
           條件。

提名董事的程序及流程                 Procedures and Process for Nomination of Directors
提名委員會將根據下列程序及流程就委任董        The Nomination Committee will recommend to the Board for the
事一事向董事會作出建議：               appointment of a Director in accordance with the following procedures
                           and process:


(a)   在妥為考慮該等條件下，提名委員會     (a)   The Nomination Committee may consult any source it deems
      在物色或甄選合適候選人時可向其認           appropriate in identifying or selecting suitable candidates,
      為合適的任何來源查詢，例如：由現           such as referrals from existing Directors, advertising,
      任董事轉介、刊登廣告、由第三方代           recommendations from third-party agency firm and proposals
      理人公司推薦以及由本公司的股東建           from shareholders of the Company with due consideration given
      議；                         to the Criteria;


(b)   提名委員會在評核候選人的適合程度     (b)   The Nomination Committee may adopt any process it deems
      時可採納其認為合適的任何流程，例           appropriate in evaluating the suitability of the candidates, such
      如：面試、背景查核、簡介申述及對           as interviews, background checks, presentations and third party
      於第三方轉介作出查核；                reference checks;


(c)   建議人選將會被要求提交所需的個人     (c)   The proposed candidates will be asked to submit the necessary
      資料及履歷供提名委員會作考慮之            personal information and biography for the Nomination
      用。提名委員會如認為有必要，可以           Committee’s consideration. The Nomination Committee may
      要求候選人提供額外資料及文件；            request the candidate(s) to provide additional information and
                                 documents, if considered necessary;


(d)   在考慮某名候選人是否適合擔任董事     (d)   Upon considering a candidate suitable for the directorship, the
      一職之後，提名委員會將舉行會議            Nomination Committee will hold a meeting and/or by way of

     風險管理及內部監控                                  RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
     董事會整體負責維持有效的風險管理及內部                        The Board has the overall responsibility of maintaining an effective risk
     監控系統，為重大的失實陳述或損失作出合                        management and internal control system to provide reasonable, though
     理而非絕對的保證，並管理而非消除未能達                        not absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss and to
     到業務目標的風險。上述風險包括但不限於                        manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
     與ESG有關的重大風險。                               objectives. The above risks include, but are not limited to, material risks
                                                related to ESG.

     司風險管理及內部監控系統對應的所有重大                        conducts annual review of the effectiveness of the Company’s systems
     監控的有效性進行年度檢討，包括本公司的                        of risk management and internal control covering all material controls,
     財務監控、內部監控及風險管理系統。                          including financial controls, internal control and risk management
                                                systems.


     審核與風險管理委員會應董事會的委派或主                        The Audit and Risk Management Committee considers the major

     究。                                         response to these findings.

股東權利                      SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS
股東召開股東特別大會的程序             Procedures for shareholders to convene special general meeting

根據百慕達一九八一年公司法（「公司法」）      In accordance with the Section 74 of the Companies Act 1981 of
第 74 條，於遞呈要求日期持有本公司不少     Bermuda (“Companies Act”), the shareholders of the Company holding
於（賦於本公司股東大會上之投票權）十分       at the date of the deposit of the requisition not less than one-tenth of
之一繳足股本的本公司股東有權召開本公        such of the paid-up capital of the Company carrying the right of voting
司股東特別大會。倘董事於上述要求之送        at general meetings of the Company, shall have the right to convene
達日期二十一日內未有召開會議，代表總        a special general meeting of the Company. If the directors do not
投票權半數以上的呈請人可自行召開會議。       within twenty-one days from the date of the deposit of the requisition
                          proceed duly to convene a meeting, the requisitionists representing
                          more than one half of the total voting rights may themselves convene
                          a meeting.


召開股東特別大會的書面要求可提交至本        The written requisition for the special general meeting can be lodged
公司的香港主要辦事處，地址為香港灣仔        at the Company’s principal office in Hong Kong at Room 1901-02,
港灣道26號華潤大廈1901-02室。       China Resources Building, 26 Harbour Road, Wanchai, Hong Kong.


於股東大會上提呈議案之程序             Procedures for shareholders to put forward proposals at a general
                          meeting

根據公司法第79 條及第80 條，持有本公     Pursuant to the Sections 79 and 80 of the Companies Act, either
司附帶權利可於本公司股東大會投票之繳        any number of the registered shareholders holding not less than
足股本不少於二十分之一(5%)之登記股       one-twentieth (5%) of the paid-up capital of the Company carrying the
東（「呈請人」），或不少於 100 名有關登記   right of voting at general meetings of the Company (“Requisitionists”),
股東，可向本公司提交書面要求：(a)向有      or not less than 100 of such registered shareholders, can request
權接收下一屆股東大會通告之股東發出通        the Company in writing to (a) give to shareholders entitled to receive
知，以告知任何可能於該大會上正式動議        notice of the next general meeting notice of any resolution which may
及擬於會上動議之決議案；及(b)向有權獲      properly be moved and is intended to be moved at that meeting; and
發送任何股東大會通告之股東傳閱不超過        (b) circulate to shareholders entitled to have notice of any general
1,000字之陳述書，以告知於該大會上提呈     meeting any statement of not more than 1,000 words with respect to
之決議案所述事宜或將處理之事項。          the matter referred to in any proposed resolution or the business to be
                          dealt with at that meeting.


由所有呈請人簽署之呈請可由若干相同格        The requisition signed by all the Requisitionists may consist of
式之文件組成，各自須經一名或以上呈請        several documents in like form, each signed by one or more of the
人簽署；且呈請須在不少於（倘為要求通知       Requisitionists; and it must be deposited at the Registered Office
的決定議案之呈請）大會舉行前六週或（倘       with a sum reasonably sufficient to meet the Company’s relevant
為任何其他呈請）大會舉行前一週，遞交至       expenses, not less than six weeks before the meeting in case of a
註冊辦事處，並須支付足以彌補本公司相        requisition requiring notice of a resolution or not less than one week
關開支之款項。惟倘在遞交呈請後六週或        before the meeting in the case of any other requisition. Provided that if
較短期間內之某一日召開股東週年大會，        an AGM is called for a date six weeks or less after the requisition has
則該呈請雖未有在規定時間內遞交，就此        been deposited, the requisition though not deposited within the time
而言亦將被視為已妥為遞交。             required shall be deemed to have been properly deposited for the
                          purposes thereof.

                                                meeting.

     事會推選外，退任董事以外之任何人士概                         other than a retiring Director, shall, unless recommended by the
     不符合資格於任何股東大會上膺選董事一                         Board for election, be eligible for election to the office of Director
     職，除非表明有意提名該人士膺選董事之                         at any general meeting, unless notice in writing of the intention to
     書面通知及該人士願意參選之書面通知已                         propose that person for election as a Director and notice in writing by

長期獎勵計劃                      LONG TERM AWARD SCHEME
於二零零八年十二月一日，本公司採納一          On 1st December, 2008, the Company adopted a share repurchased
項股份購回現金獎勵計劃（「獎勵計劃」），        cash award scheme (the “Incentive Award Scheme”), pursuant to
據此，本集團或投資公司的經甄選僱員皆          which selected employees of the Group or investee companies (being
有權根據計劃規則參加獎勵計劃。投資公          any company in which the Company directly or indirectly owns 5%
司乃指任何一家由本公司直接或間接擁有          or more attributable equity interests (the “Investee Company”), and
5%或以上歸屬權益的公司（「投資公司」），       in calculating the percentage interest so attributed, the percentage
就計算歸屬權益百分比而言，在投資公司          interest held in the Investee Company shall be multiplied by the
所持有權益的百分比須乘以本集團透過其          percentage interest in each level of entity through which such interest
他公司持有該權益的該等中間公司的百分          is held by the Group), are entitled to participate in the Incentive Award
比。                          Scheme pursuant to the scheme rules.



獎勵計劃旨在嘉許若干僱員的貢獻及鼓勵          The purpose of the Incentive Award Scheme is to recognise and
彼等作出貢獻，並提供獎勵及協助本集團          motivate the contribution of certain employees and to provide
及投資公司挽留其現有僱員及延聘更多僱          incentives and to enable the Group and the Investee Company in
員，並就達到本公司的長期商業目標向彼          retaining their existing employees and recruiting additional employees
等提供直接經濟利益。除非被董事會提前          and to provide them with a direct economic interest in attaining
終止，獎勵計劃自採納日期起十年內有           the long-term business objectives of the Company. Unless early
效。根據獎勵計劃授出獎勵股份及於其整          termination by the Board, the Incentive Award Scheme shall be valid
個有效期內授予經甄選僱員獎勵股份的總          and effective for a term of 10 years commencing on the adoption
數上限分別以本公司不時已發行股本的5%         date. The maximum aggregate numbers of award shares which can
及0.5%為限。                    be awarded under the Incentive Award Scheme and to a selected
                            employee throughout its duration are limited to 5% and 0.5% of the
                            issued share capital of the Company from time to time respectively.


根據獎勵計劃規則，本公司已委託中銀國          Pursuant to the rules of the Incentive Award Scheme, the Company
際英國保誠信託有限公司為受託人（「受託         has appointed BOCI-Prudential Trustee Limited as trustee (the
人」），據此，受託人將於市場上以本集團         “Trustee”), pursuant to which existing shares will be purchased by
授出的現金購入現有股份，並以信託形式          the Trustee from the market out of cash contributed by the Group and
代相關經甄選僱員持有，直至該等股份按          be held in trust for the relevant selected employees until such shares
獎勵計劃的條文歸屬該相關經甄選僱員為          are vested with the relevant selected employees in accordance with
止。受託人於二零零九年四月七日以總成          the provisions of the Incentive Award Scheme. A total of 70,000,000
本約253,534,680 港元（包括交易成本）購   shares were purchased at an aggregate cost of approximately

     核數師就審計綜合財務報表須承擔的責任                         Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated
                                                financial statements

     我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否                         Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the
     不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤                         consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material
     陳述取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見                         misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s
     的核數師報告。我們是按照百慕達1981 年                      report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as
     《公司法案》第90 條的規定，僅向整體股東                      a body, in accordance with Section 90 of the Bermuda Companies
     報告。除此以外，我們的報告不可用作其                         Act 1981, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility
     他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，對任                         towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this
     何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。                            report.


     合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按                         Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a
     照《香港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重                        guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will
     大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可                         always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements
     以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們                         can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,
     單獨或滙總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使                         individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to
     用者依賴財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則                         influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these
     有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                             consolidated financial statements.


     在 根 據《 香 港 審 計 準 則 》進 行 審 計 的 過 程          As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
     中，我們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷                         professional judgement and maintain professional skepticism
     疑態度。我們亦：                                   throughout the audit. We also:


     •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                       •    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
         合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風                             consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,
         險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些                            design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,
         風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑                            and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate
         證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐                            to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a
         可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛                            material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for
         假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因                            one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery,
         此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤                            intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of
         陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導                            internal control.
         致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。


     •   了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計                       •    Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit
         適當的審計程序，但目的並非對              貴               in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate in the
         集團內部控制的有效性發表意見。                             circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an opinion

     •   評價董事所採用會計政策的恰當性及                       •    Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used and the

1. 一般資料                            1. GENERAL
  本公司為一家於百慕達註冊成立的獲                    The Company is a listed public company incorporated in
  豁免的上市有限公司，其股份於香港                    Bermuda as an exempted company with limited liability and its
  聯 合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司（「 聯 交 所 」）上       shares are listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited
  市，其中間母公司為於香港註冊成立                    (the “Stock Exchange”). The Company’s intermediate parent
  的華潤（集團）有限公司（「華潤集團公                  company is China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited
  司」）；而其最終控股公司為中國華潤                   (“CRH”), a company incorporated in Hong Kong, and its ultimate
  有限公司（「中國華潤」），該公司為一                  holding company is China Resources Company Limited (“CRCL”),
  家於中國成立的公司。本公司註冊辦                    a company established in the PRC. The addresses of the
  事處及主要營業地點的地址於本年報                    registered office and principal place of business of the Company
  內公司資料一節披露。                          are disclosed in the Corporate Information of the annual report.


  本公司為一家投資控股公司，其主要                    The Company is an investment holding company. The activities
  附屬公司、合營公司及聯營公司的業                    of its principal subsidiaries, joint ventures and associates are set
  務分別載於附註47、48及49。                    out in notes 47, 48 and 49, respectively.


  綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，                  The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong
  原因為本公司為一家股份於香港聯交                    Kong dollars (“HK$”) as the Company is a public company with
  所上市的上市公司，而其大部分投資                    its shares listed on the Stock Exchange in Hong Kong where
  者亦在香港，因此本公司董事認為在                    most of its investors are located, and therefore, the directors of
  呈列本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集                   the Company consider that HK$ is preferable in presenting the
  團」）經營業績及財務狀況時使用港元                   operating results and financial position of the Company and its
  更為合適。                               subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”).


2. 編製基準                            2. BASIS OF PREPARATION
  於編製本綜合財務報表時，有見                      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors
  及於二零二三年十二月三十一日                      of the Company have given careful consideration to the future
  本集團的流動負債超逾其流動資產                     liquidity of the Group in light of the fact that the Group’s current
  15,655,135,000港元及本集團有資本             liabilities exceeded its current assets by HK$15,655,135,000,
  承擔436,775,000 港元，本公司董事              and the Group had capital commitments of HK$436,775,000 as
  已審慎考慮本集團的未來流動資金狀                    at 31st December, 2023. As at 31st December, 2023, the Group
  況。於二零二三年十二月三十一日，                    had bank and other borrowings and medium-term notes totalling
  本集團的銀行及其他借貸及中期票                     of HK$22,723,798,000, of which HK$4,892,674,000 was classified
  據合共 22,723,798,000 港元，其中            as current liabilities.

  該等修訂縮小了初始確認豁免的範            The amendments narrow the scope of the initial recognition
  圍，使其不適用於在初始確認時產生           exemption such that it does not apply to transactions that give
  等額及抵銷暫時性差異的交易，如租           rise to equal and offsetting temporary differences on initial
  賃及退役債務。就租賃及退役債務而           recognition such as leases and decommissioning liabilities. For
  言，相關遞延稅項資產及負債須於所           leases and decommissioning liabilities, the associated deferred
  呈列最早比較期間開始起確認，而任           tax assets and liabilities are required to be recognised from the
  何累計影響則確認為於該日對保留盈           beginning of the earliest comparative period presented, with
  利或其他權益部分的調整。就所有其           any cumulative effect recognised as an adjustment to retained
  他交易而言，該等修訂被應用於所呈           earnings or other components of equity at that date. For all other
  列最早期間開始後發生的該等交易。           transactions, the amendments are applied to those transactions
                             that occur after the beginning of the earliest period presented.

  歷史成本一般以交換貨品及服務時給          Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the
  予代價的公平值為基準。               consideration given in exchange for goods and services.


  公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有          Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset
  秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負          or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
  債須支付的價格，而不論該價格是否          market participants at the measurement date, regardless of
  直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估          whether that price is directly observable or estimated using
  計。若市場參與者於計量日期對資產          another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value
  或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債的特          of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the
  點，則本集團於估計資產或負債的公          characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants would
  平值時會考慮該等特點。此等綜合財          take those characteristics into account when pricing the asset
  務報表中作計量及 ╱ 或披露用途的公        or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for measurement
  平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財          and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated financial

    倘本集團於投資對象的投票權未能佔      When the Group has less than a majority of the voting rights
    大多數，但只要投票權足以賦予本集      of an investee, it has power over the investee when the voting
    團實際能力可單方面掌控投資對象之      rights are sufficient to give it the practical ability to direct the

    有權力。一般而言，推定多數投票權      a presumption that the majority of voting rights results in control.
    形成控制權。在評估本集團於投資對      The Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in
    象的投票權是否足以賦予其權力時，      assessing whether or not the Group’s voting rights in an investee

4. 重大會計政策（續）           4. M A T E R I A L A C C O U N T I N G P O L I C I E S
                          (CONTINUED)
  金融資產減值                  Impairment of financial assets

  本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益        The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
  持有的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損         (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
 （「預期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸       profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
  虧損乃基於根據合同到期的合同現金        contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and
  流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流        all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted
  量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利        at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The
  率的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包        expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of
  括出售所持抵押的現金流量或組成合        collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to
  同條款的其他信貸提升措施。           the contractual terms.


 一般方法                     General approach

  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自        ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
  初步確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險        which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk
  而言，就未來12 個月內可能發生的       since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
  違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12 個月      that result from default events that are possible within the next
  預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。        12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for
  就自初步確認起已經顯著增加的信貸        which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since
  風險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘        initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
  下風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計        expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of
  提虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸虧        the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
  損）。


  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具        At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit
  的信貸風險自初步確認起是否出現顯        risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since
  著增加。作此評估時，本集團比較金        initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group
  融工具於報告日期出現違約的風險與        compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial
  該金融工具於初步確認日期出現違約        instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default
  的風險，並考慮毋須花費不必要成本        occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial
  或精力即可獲得的合理及有理據的資        recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
  料，包括過往及前瞻性資料。本集團        information that is available without undue cost or effort,
  認為倘合同付款逾期超過30 天時信貸      including historical and forward-looking information. The Group
  風險已顯著增加。                considers that there has been a significant increase in credit risk
                          when contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

 簡化法                       Simplified approach

  就並無重大融資組成部分的或本集團         For trade receivables and assets related to contract works that
  應用可行權宜方法不就重大融資組成         do not contain a significant financing component or when the
  部分的影響作出調整的應收貿易賬款         Group applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect

  用簡化法計算預期信貸虧損。根據簡         simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified

  期預期信貸虧損確認虧損撥備。本集         at each reporting date. The Group has established a provision

  瞻性因素及經濟環境作出調整。           the economic environment.

  稅項涉及於其他全面收益或直接在權       except when they relate to items that are recognised in other
  益確認的項目，則即期及遞延稅項亦       comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,
  會分別於其他全面收益或直接於權益       the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other
  內確認。倘對業務合併進行初步會計       comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively. Where
  處理時產生即期或遞延稅項，則對業       current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial accounting for a
  務合併進行會計處理時會計入該稅項       business combination, the tax effect is included in the accounting
  影響。                    for the business combination.


  股息                     Dividends

  倘末期股息於股東大會上獲股東批        Final dividends are recognised as a liability when they are
  准，則末期股息確認為負債。建議末       approved by the shareholders in a general meeting. Proposed
  期股息於財務報表附註中披露。         final dividends are disclosed in the notes to the financial
                         statements.


  中期股息同步建議及宣派，乃因本公       Interim dividends are simultaneously proposed and declared,
  司備忘錄及組織章程細則授權董事宣       because the Company’s memorandum and articles of association
  派中期股息。因此，中期股息於其建       grant the directors the authority to declare interim dividends.
  議及宣派時隨即確認為負債。          Consequently, interim dividends are recognised immediately as a
                         liability when they are proposed and declared.

  貨幣港元呈列。                presented in HK$, which is the Company’s functional currency

  以該實體功能貨幣以外的貨幣（外幣）      entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional

  換算確認。於報告期末，以外幣計值       rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions.
  的貨幣項目均按該日的適用匯率重新       At the end of the reporting period, monetary items denominated
  換算。以外幣為單位按公平值入賬的       in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing
  非貨幣項目，按釐定公平值日期的適       at that date. Non-monetary items carried at fair value that are
  用匯率重新換算。以外幣歷史成本計       denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates
  量的非貨幣項目則不會重新換算。        prevailing on the date when the fair value was determined. Non-

5. 關鍵性會計判斷及估計不明      5. C R I T I C A L A C C O U N T I N G J U D G E M E N T S
   朗因素的主要來源             AND KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION
                        UNCERTAINTY
 於應用本集團的會計政策（如附註4所       In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are
 述）時，本公司董事須就並未在其他        described in note 4, the directors of the Company are required to
 來源顯示的資產及負債的賬面值作出        make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying
 判斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關        amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent
 假設乃根據過往經驗及被視為相關的        from other sources. The estimates and associated assumptions
 其他因素作出。實際結果或會有別於        are based on historical experience and other factors that are
 該等估計。                   considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
                         estimates.


 有關估計及相關假設會作持續檢討。        The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on
 倘對會計估計的修訂僅影響對估計進        an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are
 行修訂的期間，則於該期間確認，或        recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if the
 倘修訂會影響目前及未來期間，則會        revision affects only that period, or in the period of the revision
 於作出修訂期間及未來期間確認。         and future periods if the revision affects both current and future
                         periods.

  約。通常已提前作出一次性付款以                                  sum payments were generally made upfront to acquire the leased

  年，而根據該等土地租賃的條款，將                                 ongoing payments will be made under the terms of these land

  通常為 2 至 25 年，而廠房、機器及設                            2 and 25 years, and leases of plant, machinery and equipment
  備的租期通常介乎2至24年。                                   have lease terms between 2 and 24 years.

      36. 長期獎勵計劃                                 36. LONG TERM AWARD SCHEME
          於二零零八年十二月一日，本公司採                            On 1st December, 2008, the Company adopted the share
          納 股 份 購 回 現 金 獎 勵 計 劃（「 獎 勵 計               repurchased cash award scheme (“Incentive Award Scheme”)
          劃」），作為挽留並鼓勵員工的獎勵，                           as an incentive to retain and encourage the employees for the
          以實現本集團持續運營及發展。                              continual operation and development of the Group.


          根據獎勵計劃，本公司現有股份將由                            Pursuant to the Incentive Award Scheme, existing shares of
          受託人利用本集團所注入的現金從市                            the Company will be purchased by the trustee from the market
          場購買，並以信託方式為特定僱員持                            out of cash contributed by the Group and be held in trust for
          有，直至股份歸屬於特定僱員為止。                            the selected employees until such shares are vested with the
          倘計劃有效期內所授出限制性股份總                            selected employees. No restricted shares will be granted if the
          數超過本公司於計劃採納日期已發行                            aggregate number of restricted shares awarded throughout
          股本之 5%，則不得再授出限制性股                           the duration of the scheme is to be in excess of 5% of the
          份。根據獎勵計劃可授予特定僱員的                            issued share capital of the Company at the adoption date of the
          限制性股份的最大數目不得超過本公                            scheme. The maximum number of restricted shares which may
          司不時已發行股本之0.5%。                              be awarded to a selected employee under the Incentive Award
                                                      Scheme shall not exceed 0.5% of the issued share capital of the
                                                      Company from time to time.


          於二零一零年三月十六日，本公司對                            On 16th March, 2010, the Company amended the Incentive
          獎勵計劃作出修訂，將用現金取代受                            Award Scheme such that cash instead of restricted shares will be
          限制股份作為獎勵。本公司於根據獎                            awarded. The Company will utilise the proceeds generated from
          勵計劃作出現金獎勵時，將動用出售                            disposal of the restricted shares held by the trustee for making

      42. 資本風險管理                                 42. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
          本集團管理其資本以維持經營業務所                            The Group manages its capital to maintain a balance between
          得現金流量的持續性及使用銀行及其                            continuity of cash flows from operating activities and the flexibility
          他借貸靈活性之間的平衡。本集團亦                            through the use of bank and other borrowings. The Group also
          定期監控現時及預期流動資金需求及                            monitors the current and expected liquidity requirements and
          符合貸款契諾，以確保具備足夠的營                            its compliance with lending covenants regularly to ensure that
          運資金及充足的承諾融資渠道以滿足                            sufficient working capital and adequate committed lines of
          流動資金需求。本集團維持過往年度                            funding are maintained to meet its liquidity requirements. The
          之整體策略不變。                                    Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior years.


          本集團的資本架構包括負債（包括銀                            The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which
          行及其他借貸）及優先票據，不包括                            include bank and other borrowings, senior notes, net of bank
          銀行結餘及本公司擁有人應佔權益                             balances and equity attributable to owners of the Company,
         （由已發行資本、保留溢利及其他儲備                            comprising issued capital, retained profits and other reserves.
          組成）。


          本集團管理層定期檢討資本架構。管                            The management of the Group reviews the capital structure on a
          理層認為資本成本及相關風險乃檢討                            regular basis. As part of this review, management considers the
          內容的一部分，並採取適當行動調整                            cost of capital and the associated risks, and takes appropriate
          本集團的資本架構，包括派付股息、                            actions to adjust the Group’s capital structure through the
          發行新股、發行新債或償清現有債務。                           payment of dividends, new share issues as well as the issue of
                                                      new debts or the redemption of existing debts.

          (c)   金融工具的公平值計量（續）                         (c)      Fair value measurements of financial instruments
                                                            (Continued)

                本集團以經常性基準按公平值計                              Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that are measured
                量的金融資產的公平值（續）                               at fair value on a recurring basis (Continued)

                公平值計量根據公平值計量的輸                                 Fair value measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2, or
                入數據可觀察程度及公平值計量                                 3 based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value
                的輸入數據對其整體的重要性分                                 measurements are observable and the significance of the
                類為第一級、第二級或第三級，                                 inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which
                詳情如下：                                          are described as follows:


                (i)     第一級輸入數據是實體於                            (i)     Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
                        計量日期可以取得的相同                                    markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity
                        資產或負債於活躍市場之                                    can access at the measurement date;
                        報價（未經調整）；


                (ii)    第二級輸入數據是就資產                            (ii)    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices
                        或負債直接或間接地可觀                                    included within Level 1, that are observable for the
                        察之輸入數據（第一級內包                                   asset or liability, either directly or indirectly;
                        括的報價除外）；


                (iii)   第三級輸入數據是資產或                            (iii)   Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or
                        負債的不可觀察輸入數據。                                   liability.


                於本年度及過往年度，第一級、                                 There were no transfers among Levels 1, 2 and 3 in the
                第二級及第三級之間並無任何轉                                 current and prior years.
                撥。

                表按攤餘成本入賬的其他金融資                                 amounts of other financial assets and financial liabilities
                產及金融負債（附註32 所披露的                               (except for senior notes as disclosed in note 32) recorded
                優先票據除外）的賬面值與彼等                                 at amortised cost in the consolidated financial statements

          由於相關業務的決策均需要本集團及                                              The Group is able to exercise joint control over these companies

          該等公司行使共同控制權。故此，該                                              the unanimous consent of the Group and the joint ventures.
          等公司被視為本集團的合營公司。                                               Accordingly, these companies are regarded as joint ventures of
                                                                        the Group.
